- **2026-05-31 (Functional UI verification of the data fixes + a 2nd pre-existing bug fixed — the Balance-Sheet tab had been 500-ing for ~5 days)** — Closed the loop on this session's data work by proving it reaches the SCREEN, not just the DB. New `e2e/smoke/verify-ebitda-bs-cf-render.spec.ts` (Playwright, `loginAs` fixture, 3 tests, all green) with concrete aria/text evidence — no aesthetics (owner=user). **#2 terminal HeatMap:** activating AZSEKER-EDEN renders the cell `aria-label="AZSEKER-EDEN IND_EBITDA_MARGIN green ●"` (the corrected +169.77%, replacing the bogus "−738%"-class red) + asserts the old red is gone; activating AZSEKER-CPC renders `IND_EBITDA_MARGIN red` (source-faithful +9.29%, below the green floor) — the HeatMap shows the ACTIVE company's row, so each entity is checked in turn. **#1 BS + CF tabs:** both render data (BS "Total Assets" card; CF monthly table with Inflows/Outflows columns + ≥4 rows) — not the empty state. **#3 AI briefs:** env-gated (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, like the pilot spec's AI-Explain exclusion); covered transitively — the brief reads the same `IndicatorValue` (169.77 green) this spec confirms, and the variance/brief unit tests lock the forwarding. **2nd pre-existing bug found + fixed (this is why #1 BS initially failed):** `GET /api/budgeting/balance-sheet` was returning **500** for every plan — its `orderBy` referenced the scalar `accountCode`, a column DROPPED in Phase 2.1 (2026-05-26, → `accountId` + `account` FK), so Prisma threw "Unknown argument `accountCode`. Did you mean `accountId`?" → the component fell to its empty state ("No Balance Sheet data available") for ~5 days. The handler-test prisma mock ignores `orderBy`, so it stayed green — only hitting the live route surfaced it (the "verify one layer up" lesson, again). **Fix:** order by the relation (`{ account: { code: "asc" } }`) + `include: { account: { select: { code, name } } }` so the client can label detail rows — `accountName` was *also* dropped, collapsing the per-account breakdown into one blank row; restored via `account.name ?? account.code`. Reproduced both the failure and the fix against the live DB before editing (accountCode → error; account.code → 251 rows: asset 126 / equity 51 / liability 74). **Verified live:** balance-sheet API 500 → 200; all 3 e2e green; the LaunchAgent dev server needed a restart to drop its cached error-module. tsc 0; full suite 5465.

A CFO opening the BS or CF tab would have seen roughly doubled totals. **Root-caused the whole class** (not one instance) by auditing every CF/BS/budgetLine read in `src/app/api` + `src/lib`. **Fixed — all PASSIVE financial-display reads:** `GET /api/budgeting/balance-sheet`, `GET /api/budgeting/cash-flow` (current + prior-year opening-balance read), `GET /api/budgeting/cash-flow/odds`, `GET /api/budgeting/availability` (the 3 soft-delete count surfaces), `report-engine.ts` (custom report builder — model-aware `deletedAt:null` for budgetLine/balanceSheetLine/cashFlowEntry entity types, flows into both the distinct-codes scan and the main query), `company-financials-snapshot.ts` (consumed by the scheduler-driven intel crossing-scan). **Verified already-correct (no change needed):** the terminal recompute path (`recompute-data-source.ts:328` budgetLine + `:175` balanceSheetLine both filter `deletedAt:null` — Phase 7.M Step 4), and `pnl` + `analytics` routes — which is *why* the terminal EBITDA (#2) and P&L statement were correct all along. **Tests:** new `GET /api/budgeting/cash-flow` regression (asserts `deletedAt:null` on both year reads) + updated the org-scope assertions in the balance-sheet / odds / availability handler tests to include the filter; report-engine + reports preview/export suites green unchanged. tsc 0; **full suite 5465 passing** (+2). Convention matches the pre-existing `plans/route.ts` (which already filtered). **Deferred to a documented phase-2 follow-up (CARRYOVER 2026-05-31, owner=dev):** the remaining reads are write/export/enumeration paths — `csv-template` (export), `plans/[id]/apply-templates` + `plans/[id]/create-version` + `cash-flow/generate` (write/version — risk = copying archived rows into NEW records), `period-snapshot` (planId enumeration), `completeness-checker` (onboarding-presence counts) — none are passive CFO-facing financial displays, so they were not blocking this verification.

- **2026-05-31 (EBITDA fix — was showing NET margin; the user's «ты на 100% уверен?» caught it)** — Pushed past "looks verified" and cross-checked the system's DERIVED P&L subtotals against the source's OWN total rows (PLF.03 gross / PLF.08 EBITDA / PLF.10 net — author-computed, which breaks the verifier's parser-circularity). revenue/cogs/gross/net matched, **but EBITDA was wrong for ALL 4 entities**: the budgetLine resolver computed `ebitda = net_income + da_total`, but `da_total=0` (PLF-format has no SAP D&A codes) AND the `expense` lineType lumps D&A + interest + tax, so "EBITDA" collapsed to NET. CPC showed 4.55% (net) vs the source's own EBITDA 9.29%; EDEN a bogus deeply-negative figure vs its real +170% (the long-standing "EDEN −738%" anomaly). The audit ALSO surfaced EDEN's opex under-captured by 34.5k (PLF.05 nested-leaf double-count +1.38M + PLF.07 income misclassified as expense — the leaf-derivation is structurally unreliable for complex sheets; revenue/cogs/gross are immune since they don't depend on it). **Fix:** capture the source's OWN monthly EBITDA subtotal (PLF.08) as `pl_ebitda` operational_facts (`scripts/capture-plf-ebitda.ts`, idempotent + DRY); the budgetLine resolver SUMS them per period for `context.ebitda` (flow semantics), falling back to `net+da_total` when none exist (guarded for mock DS). **Verified live:** IND_EBITDA_MARGIN now = source for all 4 (CPC 9.29% / AZSF −17.68% / EDEN +169.77% [green, was bogus red] / MALT −3.94%); composite + financial-stress sub-composite + AI briefs auto-correct (they read the indicator). tsc 0; full suite 5463. Commit `339aa5d`. **Follow-ups:** ✅ (1) pl_ebitda capture wired into `makePlfHandler` (`parsePlfEbitdaSubtotal`) — future P&L re-imports auto-capture (commit `0558a91`); ✅ (3) `verify-azseker-vs-source.ts` EBITDA chain-check added (source PLF.08 vs DB IND_EBITDA_MARGIN×revenue, all 4 ✓, commit `d2abc47`); 🟡 (2) EDEN net 34.5k residual — DEFERRED as cosmetic (1.8% of EDEN net; net margin deeply negative either way → no status change; root = PLF.05 deep-nesting leaf-sum≠parent + PLF.07 income-as-expense; clean fix is non-trivial/risky for a non-material figure — revisit if EDEN opex-detail becomes load-bearing).

- **2026-05-31 (Full-project data verification vs source files — caught + fixed 2 import bugs, restored 478 lost rows; financial core now bit-perfect)** — Per user «сверяй с файлами … тщательно а не сверху» built `scripts/verify-azseker-vs-source.ts` — reuses the import adapters (`parsePlfPlSheet` / `parseWorkbookBsSheet` / `parsePlfCfSheet`) on the CURRENT source `Guvven Fin.xlsx` and reconciles per company / type / MONTH + annual against LIVE rows (`deletedAt:null`). **Result: P&L + BS + CF all bit-perfect (maxMonthΔ=0) for all 4 entities (CPC/AZSF/EDEN/MALT).** Getting there surfaced + fixed real issues: **(1) Two import-batch cross-archive bugs** — `bs-import-batch` scoped its soft-delete reset by `planId` only, `cf-import-batch` by a shared `sourceTag`; since every AzerSheker entity shares one plan + one sourceTag, each per-entity multi-file batch archived the previously-written siblings' live rows → only the LAST entity (MALT) survived. The 2026-05-26 multi-import had thus left CPC/AZSF/EDEN with ZERO live balance sheet + cash flow (statements blank in-app). P&L was immune (its reset correctly scopes by `companyId`). Fixed both to scope by entity (`companyId` for BS, `sourceId` prefix for CF) incl. the recon reads; +4 regression tests on shared-plan/shared-sourceTag isolation (commits `93ca122`, `8649835`). **(2) Restored the lost data** via the PRODUCTION handlers (`makeBsHandler`/`makeCfHandler` → fixed batches) re-parsing `Guvven Fin.xlsx` — 206 BS + 272 CF rows; each batch logged `archived=0` (proving the fix) + recompute clean (commits `0e0d42a`, `ff5d5f7`; `scripts/reimport-azseker-bs.ts`, DRY + MODE=bs|cf|both). **Indicators confirmed reflecting the verified source** (CPC IND_REVENUE_TOTAL = 6,475,883 = source; FP_GROSS_MARGIN 23.43%; EBITDA 4.55%). **Caught my own false positive** — a first pass showed «6× inflation» because the verifier summed soft-deleted ghost rows; `deletedAt:null` fixed it (no data bug). **Non-critical findings resolved:** ops_facts suffered NO loss (KPI batch hard-deletes per `companyId` — no cross-archive); `FP_INVENTORY_TURNS` is correctly `unknown` (only BS-derived indicator; annual turns need a year-end balance, source is Q1-only — confirmed via MALT which always had BS yet is still unknown; a latest-month fallback was REJECTED — it would understate turns + misclassify risk). **Remaining (lower-stakes, import-time-verified):** per-metric ops-values + other source files (court/audit/risk/land/CAPEX/forward).

- **2026-05-31 (Prod `migrate deploy` path VERIFIED end-to-end + a 2nd pg_dump-ism fixed — `migrate dev` had NOT surfaced it)** — Validated the baseline's headline benefit (clean fresh-DB deploys) by running the ACTUAL prod command against a throwaway DB: `DATABASE_URL=<fresh> prisma migrate deploy`. **Caught a real bug:** the init applied the full schema (60 tables + 56 RLS + 7 enums) but `migrate deploy` then failed **P1014** ("`_prisma_migrations` does not exist") — pg_dump emits `SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path','',false)` which **empties the session search_path**, so after the init Prisma can't resolve its own unqualified `_prisma_migrations` bookkeeping table to record the migration. `migrate dev`'s shadow flow dodged this (different code path), so only testing the real prod command caught it — a first production deploy would otherwise have left a half-built, unrecorded schema. **Fix:** replaced that one line in the init with `SET search_path = public;` (keeps public in scope for both the init's object creation AND the post-init migration record). Re-synced the local real DB's recorded checksum (delete init row + `resolve --applied` → Prisma recomputes from the edited file; schema/data untouched). **Re-verified:** fresh-DB `migrate deploy` now applies cleanly ("All migrations have been successfully applied" — 60 tables / 56 RLS / 7 enums / 1 recorded), and real DB `migrate status` = "up to date". The full prod deploy path is now proven end-to-end on a throwaway fresh DB: provision → `migrate deploy` (60 tables / 56 RLS / 7 enums, recorded) → `seed-industries` (14) → `seed-indicators` (110 definitions) → ready for client-data import (companies=0 until import, as expected). Only the actual VM/secrets execution remains owner=user.

- **2026-05-31 (Prisma migration history REBASELINED — `migrate dev` unblocked; the earlier "RLS role lacks CREATEDB" diagnosis was WRONG)** — Investigated the `migrate dev` shadow-replay blocker end-to-end on user approval («да возьмись» → baseline). **Real root cause — NOT a CREATEDB/role issue (correcting the entry below):** the migration *history* was systemically incomplete. Multiple tables (`indicator_disclosures`, the ai_mapper/token/variance/predictive models, …) had been created via `prisma db push` and never captured in migration files, and one migration (`20260510210000`) FK-referenced a non-existent `"organizations"` table (real table is `"Organization"` — the model has no `@@map`). So the history could not replay from scratch (breaks `migrate dev` AND a fresh `migrate deploy`; existing DBs were fine only because their `_prisma_migrations` rows said *applied*). **Fix = baseline/squash:** generated ONE init migration from a `pg_dump --schema-only` of the REAL DB — deliberately NOT from `migrate diff`, because Prisma does not model the **56 RLS policies** (Phase 5.2) and a schema-only init would have silently dropped row-level security from every future deploy. Stripped pg_dump-16's `\restrict`/`\unrestrict` psql meta-commands (Prisma's engine runs SQL directly, not through psql). **Validated before any destructive step** by replaying the candidate init through Prisma's *actual* migration engine against a throwaway scratch DB (`migrate diff --from-migrations <init> --to-schema-datamodel --shadow-database-url <scratch>` → clean, exit 0). Placed as `prisma/migrations/00000000000000_init` (56 RLS policies, 60 tables, 7 enums, 2 fns, 2 triggers); archived the 111 broken migration dirs to `prisma/migrations_archive_pre_baseline_20260531/` (a sibling dir Prisma ignores); backed up `_prisma_migrations` (114 rows) then `DELETE` + `resolve --applied 00000000000000_init`. **Verified:** `migrate status` = "Database schema is up to date!"; `migrate dev` now shadow-replays the init cleanly (was the P3006/P1014 failure); the real DB schema/data/RLS are UNTOUCHED (`resolve` runs no DDL — re-checked live: RLS 56/56, companies 8, IVs 1194, intel 28). **Pre-existing drift surfaced (not hidden):** `migrate dev` cleanly generates a 5-statement reconciliation for 2 FKs where the DB is stricter than the committed schema (`cash_flow_entries.accountId` NOT NULL in DB vs `String?`+`SetNull` in schema; `balance_sheet_lines.companyId` onDelete) — left for a user product decision (CARRYOVER 2026-05-31), not auto-applied. **RESOLVED same day (user chose B — tighten schema→NOT NULL):** reconciled `schema.prisma` TO the DB (`CashFlowEntry.accountId String` + `onDelete: Restrict, onUpdate: Cascade`; `BalanceSheetLine.company onUpdate: NoAction`); `migrate dev` confirms **in-sync** (empty migration — DB was already NOT NULL, so zero DB change). `tsc` then surfaced the real cost of NOT NULL — 3 code paths create account-less `CashFlowEntry` rows — all fixed: (1) budget_line cash-flow generate now inherits `line.accountId` (BudgetLine.accountId is itself NOT NULL); (2) manual `POST /api/budgeting/cash-flow` now requires `accountId` (Zod; stale "FK still nullable at DB level" comment corrected) + a 400-contract test; (3) the invoice-derived generate path is DISABLED — an invoice has no single ChartOfAccount, and per the no-synthetic-data rule we won't fabricate one (the path was unused/0-rows + already try/catch-guarded; documented to re-enable with a real invoice→CoA mapping). Live data fully supports the constraint (309/309 cash-flow rows already carry an accountId). tsc 0; cash-flow tests 33; full suite 5459 (+1). The `BudgetLine(planId, deletedAt)` index (deferred behind this blocker below) is now addable via normal `migrate dev` (still gated PREMATURE at 8 cos).

- **2026-05-31 (Cleanup + deferred-backlog triage — matrix/preview parallelized; perf-optimizations confirmed PREMATURE; a migration-tooling blocker surfaced)** — User asked to clean up small items + run deferred tasks in parallel. **Shipped (cleanup):** extracted `src/lib/risk/concurrency.ts` (`mapWithConcurrency`) + back-ported the cap-8 parallelization to `/api/indicators/matrix/preview` (was a sequential co×indicator loop; each call still canonical `recomputeIndicator`, no writes; tsc 0; 15 tests). **Parallel agents (read-only analysis, safe on a prod repo — code-agents can't run tsc/vitest in isolated worktrees: no node_modules, npm-install blocked):** (a) a caching-strategy agent returned a strong, code-grounded verdict — **building a byCategory/byDepartment cache is PREMATURE at 8 companies**: the aggregation is light (~5 findMany + JS reduce, no GROUP BY/N+1; `cost-model/db.ts` is a zero-stub), a client-side react-query cache already invalidates on write (staleTime 30s), and Redis is off-by-default in prod. **Deferred with explicit trigger:** implement only when a byCategory call p95 > ~300ms, OR > ~40 companies, OR a plan exceeds ~5k budget_lines. (b) HeatMap virtualization is **scale-gated** on the same logic (3k cells matters at 60 cos, not 8) — deferred with the existing trigger. **Findings worth acting on later:** (1) the one justified index — `BudgetLine(planId, deletedAt)`, matching the dominant `{planId, deletedAt:null}` read shape across ~15 paths — was NOT added because **`prisma migrate dev` is currently blocked**: its shadow DB fails to replay migration `20260510210000…` ("`organizations` table does not exist") — almost certainly the restricted RLS `budgetpro_app` role lacks `CREATEDB` for the shadow DB. **Fix before any future `migrate dev`:** run migrations as `budgetpro_admin` (BYPASSRLS) or set `shadowDatabaseUrl` to an admin-owned DB. `migrate deploy` (prod) is unaffected (no shadow). (2) the FX-anchor freshness item was cosmetic (currency_rate_history 24 Apr vs cbar-official-fx 28 May — both 1.70, not even stale-flagged) → skipped as not worth churning 2 risk-class routes. **Net:** prevented 2 premature optimizations + 1 cosmetic change, delivered a real parallelization, and surfaced a migration-tooling blocker. Agents: 1 rate-limited (perf/index audit — its target index was surfaced by the caching agent anyway), 1 completed, virtualization not dispatched.

- **2026-05-31 (Crisis Brief — instant-cascade latency optimization, SHIPPED + verified live)** — The cascade had waited ~9.5s after "Запустить кризис". Two fixes: **(1) narrative off the critical path** — `?mode=drivers&narrative=0` returns sim+deltas+swing+worstHit without the slow `runCrisisBrief`; the panel fetches the narrative separately via the new `POST /api/scenarios/[id]/narrative` (from the posted summary) and fills it into the brief in the background (shows "🤖 AI-бриф генерируется…" meanwhile). **(2) parallelized the sim** — `simulateByDrivers` was ~180 SEQUENTIAL `recomputeIndicator` calls; restructured into 2 concurrency-capped phases (per-company overrides, then all pairs) via a cap-8 `mapWithConcurrency`. Each call is STILL the canonical `recomputeIndicator` (disclosure / clamp / status logic preserved — only the loop is parallel), so the numbers are byte-identical (INPUT_COST_30 still 61→58, fin-health 62→56). **Result (verified live):** direct sim ~9.5s → ~0.4s (5 flagship sims in 2.17s, 215% CPU); **click→cascade ~0.9s on a warm route** (the ~8s seen in dev is Next.js on-demand route compilation — prod is pre-compiled); the ~13s AI narrative now loads in the background, hidden behind the cascade + brief. +5 tests (4 narrative-endpoint + 1 narrative=0). tsc 0; full suite 5458. **Note:** the sim parallelization also benefits the legacy `/api/indicators/matrix/preview` pattern if back-ported (left as-is for now).

- **2026-05-30 (Crisis Brief Phase 3b — NEWS-derived signal triggers, SHIPPED + verified live; "0 news rows" was a stale myth)** — Re-verified the standing "raw news = 0 rows → news triggers data-blocked" claim (per `feedback_env_blocker_reverify`) — **FALSE**: `intel_items` holds **20 real curated news items** (Report.az / Trend / BelTA / АЗЕРТАДЖ / Caliber.az …) with sentiment + industry/company tags, latest 17 May. So Phase 3b is NOT blocked. **New:** `detectNewsSignals(items)` in `scenario-signals.ts` (pure) — a materially-negative headline (sentiment ≤ −0.3) + a keyword + an industry-tag match → the implied scenario (e.g. *"Adverse weather causes AZN 13M damage to Azerbaijan agriculture"* −0.6, agro → `DROUGHT_2026`). Deterministic keyword+tag heuristic (no per-request LLM — the strip loads often), deduped to one signal per scenario (most-negative wins), conservative (needs sentiment AND keyword AND tag, so a generic negative headline never fires). `Signal.kind` added ('market' | 'news'). The `/api/scenarios/signals` route merges recent (≤30d) negative news into the response; `SignalsStrip` shows 📰 for news vs 📡 for market. **Verified LIVE** via the e2e visual run: the aria snapshot confirmed the news chip rendered — button "Adverse weather causes AZN 13M damage to Azerbaijan agriculture → DROUGHT_2026" with the 📰 marker, from a real `intel_items` row. Visual baseline regenerated (`BASELINE UPDATE: CONTENT` — news chip). tsc 0; full suite 5453 (+8). **The 3-phase "living risk radar" is now fully complete incl. news triggers** — the only acknowledged gap (raw-news ingestion) turned out already populated.

- **2026-05-30 (Crisis Brief Phase 3 — live price/weather signal triggers, SHIPPED + verified live)** — The terminal now "watches the world": a **signals strip** at the top suggests the matching crisis scenario when the live feed shows a notable condition; one click pre-selects it (same dispatch as SCN) and opens the panel. **News-derived triggers are OUT (Phase 3b — raw news = 0 rows, data-blocked).** Spec: `…specs/2026-05-30-crisis-brief-phase3-signals.md`. **New:** `scenario-signals.ts` (pure `detectSignals(feedSnapshot)` → 4 data-grounded signals with heuristic thresholds + freshness) · `GET /api/scenarios/signals` (reads FX spot+12M forward, Brent, FAO sugar, regional 14d rainfall→MIN → signals; read-only, auth-gated) · `SignalsStrip.tsx` (fetch → chips → click pre-selects + `terminal:open-scenario`; renders nothing when no signals / fetch fails). Wired into `(dashboard)/budgeting/terminal/page.tsx` above the PanelGrid. **4 signals** (all fire on the current feed): FX-forward depreciation (1.7527/1.70 = +3.1% > 1.5%) → AZN_DEVAL_15 · Brent $110.53 > $95 → BRENT_TO_140 · min rainfall 11.5 mm < 15 → DROUGHT_2026 · FAO sugar 88.5 < 90 → SUGAR_PRICE_TO_70. **Verified LIVE** via the e2e visual run (logged in, real DB/feed): the aria snapshot confirmed all 4 chips rendered with the correct labels + scenario mappings + the **stale ⚠ flag on sugar** (FAO 31 Mar). **Visual gate:** the strip shifts the viewport → `terminal-heatmap.png` regenerated (`BASELINE UPDATE: LAYOUT`) + re-verified green. Pre-select (not auto-run) — the user still pulls the trigger; thresholds are labeled heuristics; stale shown not hidden. tsc 0; full suite 5445 passing (+12). **This completes the 3-phase "living risk radar"** (Phase 1 cause-accurate engine + Phase 2 feed anchoring + Phase 3 signal triggers); the only remaining piece is Phase 3b news triggers (blocked on a fed news crawler).

- **2026-05-30 (Crisis Brief Phase 2 — live-feed anchoring + shock-from-absolute-target, SHIPPED + verified)** — Scenarios can now target an ABSOLUTE level anchored to the live feed (AZN/USD → 2.04, Brent → $140, FAO sugar → 70); the engine layer derives the B2 fraction from the current feed level (`frac = value/current − 1`) and the panel + AI narrative cite the real "current → scenario" levels with a freshness badge. Spec: `…specs/2026-05-30-crisis-brief-phase2-feed-anchoring.md`. **New:** `ScenarioShock.target {metric,value,drives}` (hasShock/readShock accept it) · `scenario-feed-context.ts` — `resolveFeedShock` (target → drives fraction, graceful fallback when the metric is missing/zero) + `resolveFeedContext` (current→scenario anchors + staleness), pure (no Date.now). **Wired:** route reads latest `currency_rate_history` + `intel_data_points` → `FeedSnapshot` (staleness vs server clock) → resolves the target BEFORE `simulateByDrivers` (engine signature unchanged — it always sees a resolved fraction) → returns `feedAnchors`; target-only scenario w/ missing feed → 422. CrisisBriefInput.feedAnchors → prompt grounds the narrative in real levels; `terminalStore.scenarioBrief` + ScenarioPanel show "📊 AZN/USD 1.7→2.04 ✓28 мая" chips. **Catalog:** AZN_DEVAL_20/15 re-authored to AZN_USD targets 2.04/1.955 (≡ the old 0.20/0.15 at the live 1.70 — proven identical), + new SUGAR_PRICE_TO_70 (FAO) + BRENT_TO_140. **Verified LIVE** (`verify-crisis-correctness.ts` now resolves the feed like the route): AZN_DEVAL_20 anchors 1.70→2.04 and yields the SAME swing as pre-Phase-2 (target≡fraction), FAO sugar correctly flagged STALE, no-writes preserved (181→181). 1:1 fraction per `drives` (no hidden transmission coefficient — the cause label + narrative carry the story); staleness shown, never hidden. tsc 0; full suite 5433 passing (+17). Engine stayed pure/unchanged — target resolution lives in the route layer. **Out (Phase 3):** auto-suggesting scenarios from feed moves; new feed scrapers.

- **2026-05-30 (What-if "Crisis Brief" Phase-1 — B2 cause-accurate scenario engine, SHIPPED + financially verified; UI built, live UX pass pending)** — Turned the Risk Terminal what-if scenario into a board-grade demo moment: one click re-derives every indicator from real economic drivers (no DB writes), cascades the HeatMap worst-first, swings the revenue-weighted holding composite, and streams a grounded AI "Crisis Brief". Full superpowers flow (brainstorm spec → plan → TDD impl); spec/plan in `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-05-30-what-if-crisis-brief*.md`. **Design pivot caught by grounding (spec §B):** the original flat-var driver schema would have produced a DUD cascade on real data — margin formulas read DERIVED scalars (`gross_profit`/`ebitda`/`net_income`, NOT primitives), `imported_input_cost=0` on every entity (so an `fx_usd` driver moves nothing), and overriding `revenue` alone moves margins the WRONG way. User chose **B2 (cause-accurate P&L recompute):** a scenario carries an economic `shock` (revenue/price/input-cost/fx/yield + `costRigidity`); the engine reads each company's baseline resolved scalars and recomputes the dependent P&L chain consistently, overriding the derived scalars. **New modules:** `scenario-shock.ts` (shock schema + P&L recompute, 13 unit) · `scenario-rederive.ts` (`simulateByDrivers` — no-writes, leaf-only revenue-weighted swing via `deriveParentComposites`, 3 unit) · `scenario-narrative.ts` (grounded AI brief mirroring variance-explainer, 4 unit) · `crisis-catalog.ts` (12 B2-shock scenarios, 3 flagships, grouped 5 categories) · `cascade-order.ts` (worst-first, 3 unit). **Extended:** `/api/scenarios/[id]/simulate?mode=drivers` (revenue sourced from `inputs.resolved.revenue`, graceful AI degrade, 3 handler tests) · `terminalStore` `scenarioBrief` slice (3 unit) · `ScenarioPanel` (🔥 crisis button, category-grouped selector, score-swing tween, line-by-line narrative, revert) · live integration test (opt-in `RUN_DB_INTEGRATION=1`). **2 CRITICAL engine bugs caught by LIVE verification:** (1) `recomputeIndicator` ALWAYS persists (`recompute.ts:805`, no skip flag) — the preview path was silently writing scenario values into period-2026 IVs → wrapped the DS so `upsertIndicatorValue` is a no-op; (2) full companies×indicators cartesian created spurious rows + simulated irrelevant pairs → iterate only existing HeatMap pairs. **DB contamination + restore (user-approved):** the first buggy run had contaminated period-2026 IVs (206→392); user chose delete + canonical recompute → pristine 181-pair baseline restored (`AZSEKER-FARM` isActive=false → correctly empty; its 24 prior IVs were stale invisible). **Economic correctness verified on LIVE DB** (`scripts/verify-crisis-correctness.ts`, no-writes proven): INPUT_COST_30 (MALT margin 19.63→−4.48, CPC 23.43→0.46 amber→red, holding 61→58) · DROUGHT_2026 (sunk-cost `costRigidity` 0.8 → MALT 19.63→−7.92, CPC 23.43→−2.82) · AZN_DEVAL_20 (FX_IMPORTED_INPUT 0→33.96% green→amber on 4 food-proc). Findings: `…specs/2026-05-30-crisis-brief-fin-correctness.md`. tsc 0 throughout; all new suites green; 12 commits pushed (`d8edfe4`..). **Remaining:** the live visual UX pass is the user's (terminal is auth-gated; developer doesn't log in) — checklist in `…specs/2026-05-30-crisis-brief-ux-review.md`. Phase 2 (live-feed anchoring) + Phase 3 (signal/news triggers) get their own specs later.

- **2026-05-30 (Panel-3 drill-down 500 fixed + `BudgetLine.category` landmine class swept)** — Live walkthrough: clicking a HeatMap cell to drill down returned **500 (empty body)**. Root cause: `indicators/values/[id]/drilldown` selected `category: true` on `budgetLine` + read `r.category`, but Phase 2.1 dropped `BudgetLine.category` (→ `accountId` FK) — Postgres "column does not exist" → uncaught 500. **tsc did NOT catch it** (same blind spot as the CF bug — the generated-client select-typing didn't flag the dropped column), and mocked handler tests returned `category` from the in-memory fake regardless of select, so ONLY a real drill-down surfaced it. Swept the whole class (3 real instances; pnl/analytics/section-context cleared — those are `BudgetActual`/`BudgetAssumption`.category, which still exist): **(1)** drill-down — dropped the `category` select; response keeps a `category` field sourced from the CoA code for UI back-compat. **(2)** `budget-lines/monthly-series` — the `{category: category!}` WHERE branch (legacy fallback when no `accountCode`) → now `{account: {code: category!}}` (resolve via the CoA FK). **(3)** `budgeting/lines/[id]` PATCH — the `data: {...category}` write would 500 on editing a line; dropped it (re-categorizing = changing the account, a separate flow). **Verified LIVE:** drill-down now 200 — CPC March (2026-03) returns **60 lines, summary revenue 1,641,459 / cogs 1,268,239 / expense 243,982, byte-for-byte the DB** (also proves #9 month-bucketing returns the correct month's lines, was 0 pre-fix); 12-month series returns Jan 530,492 / Feb 765,028 / Mar 693,998 / Apr 610,461 = DB exactly, on BOTH the accountCode and the fixed category paths. Updated 1 handler-test assertion (where shape `category` → `account.code`); tsc 0; full suite 5387 passing.

- **2026-05-30 (Holding composite is now REVENUE-WEIGHTED + consistent across Panel 1/2 — fixed R48-vs-"—" + materiality)** — Live walkthrough surfaced a cross-panel inconsistency: the holding AZSEKER showed composite **R48 in the CompanyTree (Panel 1)** but **"—" in the HeatMap row (Panel 2)** — same entity, two different displays (a terminal "numbers must tie out" violation). Two separate methods: Panel 1 = unweighted mean of children's scores `(57+55+30+21+78+49)/6 = 48`; Panel 2 = `computeCompositeByCompany` over the parent's cells, which excludes synthetic rollups → no own cells → null → "—". **Both wrong in their own way:** the unweighted mean is financially naive — it let a **0-revenue shell (PROMALT, R78 "awaiting data")** inflate the holding as a full 1/6, and the blank "—" gives no holding signal. **User chose the financially-correct fix (materiality weighting):** new shared helper `deriveParentComposites(companies, leafById)` rolls each parent up as a **revenue-weighted mean** of its children's composites (`Σ(score×revenue)/Σrevenue`; falls back to unweighted only when all child revenues are 0; fixpoint walk for multi-level holdings) — same principle as a cap-weighted index. Both panels now call the SAME helper → identical number everywhere. The matrix route surfaces per-company `revenue` (max of any IV's `resolved.revenue`) as the weight; 0-revenue entities get 0 weight and drop out. **Verified LIVE:** AZSEKER now shows **R45 in BOTH** Panel 1 (`▲R45`) and Panel 2 (`▲45`) — was 48/"—"; 45 = revenue-weighted (CPC 60% + AZSF 24% dominate; the R78 shell no longer lifts it). +4 unit tests (revenue-weighting; 0-revenue shell can't inflate; unweighted fallback; multi-level bottom-up); tsc 0; full suite 5387 passing. *Caveat (same as the rollup-value follow-up): non-financial risks aren't strictly revenue-proportional — a small-but-critical governance issue is under-weighted; acceptable default, refine later if needed.*

- **2026-05-30 (Sub-group rollup consolidates ratio indicators — fixed misleading holding EBITDA −142.9% → −29.1%)** — Caught during the live terminal↔DB walkthrough: the holding "AZSEKER" EBITDA read **−142.9%** in the AI morning brief + ТОП ХУДШИХ, but **no stored IV had that value**. Root cause in `indicators/matrix/route.ts`: the Turn-33.5 synthetic sub-group rollup computed the parent cell VALUE as a plain **average** of every child cell — including (a) EDEN's −738% EBITDA, an `out_of_range` value the system flags implausible and HIDES as `unknown` at the leaf, and (b) two no-data children counted as 0: `(−94.8 + −28.9 + −738.3 + 4.6 + 0 + 0)/6 = −142.9%`. Averaging ratios with different denominators is also mathematically wrong (the economically correct holding margin is Σebitda/Σrevenue = **−29.1%**). **Fix (user chose proper consolidation):** ratio indicators (margins, OpEx %, per-ha, FX-share — new `RATIO_ROLLUP_CONFIG`) now CONSOLIDATE `Σnumerator / Σdenominator` from the children's `inputs.resolved` P&L components, using each child's REAL components **regardless of its margin status** (EDEN's real −1.9M ebitda / 257K revenue belong in the holding Σ even though its margin is hidden). Non-ratio indicators keep the average but now EXCLUDE unknown/non-finite children (they carried no meaningful value). Status stays worst-of-children; rollups with no real contributors are dropped (honest empty, not 0/NaN). **Verified LIVE** via an authenticated matrix fetch: AZSEKER EBITDA cell now `value=-29.11` (= −2,773,565/9,527,381 ×100), status red, kind synthetic-rollup — and the AI morning brief auto-regenerated, moving its headline off the bogus "−143%" onto the real leaf value "Malt EBITDA −95%". 6 of the other 7 ТОП-ХУДШИХ figures had already matched the DB byte-for-byte (data integrity confirmed). +1 regression test (consolidation incl. an unknown-margin child; −90.9% distinguishes it from both the buggy −455% average and a naive −10% exclude-unknown average). tsc 0; full suite 5383 passing. *Follow-up noted:* absolute/count rollups (carbon scope tonnes, LEGAL_CASES counts) still average where SUM would be more correct — a future per-indicator rollup-semantics enhancement.

- **2026-05-30 (AI-Import E2E validated end-to-end + CF `category` crash fixed — `496693d`)** — Ran the multi-file AI Import (clients' primary onboarding path) against the 3 real AzerSheker workbooks via `scripts/test-multi-file-import-e2e.ts`. **Dry-run: 8/8** — AI classifier 3/3 correct (main-financial / land-registry / forward-forecast), 0 cross-file conflicts, 3 groups in correct apply-order, $0.2871 LLM (<$0.50), 68s (<120s), verdict GREEN. **`--apply` initially FAILED** — the main-financial group crashed on `Unknown field 'category' for select statement on model CashFlowEntry`: `defaultReadActualCfSums` in `cf-import-batch.ts` still selected `category`, a column Phase 2.1 dropped (→ `accountId` FK). The recon key is built from `sourceId`, so `category` was selected-but-unused (dead). **Why it slipped every gate:** the fn takes a loose `PrismaClient | Prisma.TransactionClient` union, which erodes Prisma's strict select-type inference (tsc saw nothing), and the mocked unit tests returned `category` from the in-memory fake regardless of select — so ONLY a real Postgres `--apply` surfaced it. **Fix:** dropped the dead select field + added a regression test asserting the recon select excludes `category` (tsc can't, so the unit test is the guard); `cf-import-batch` 8/8. **Re-`--apply`: 12/12** — main-financial committed 1879 rows. **Round-trip verified DB-side:** the re-import reproduced the demo data bit-equivalent — 732 live budget_lines, ALL `sortOrder == monthIndex` set **natively by the #9-fixed writer (0 backfill needed)**, AZSEKER-CPC revenue 6,475,883 (unchanged), 4 distinct months, 1219 IVs (recompute fired). This conclusively answers the standing "delete-all + reimport-from-folder → everything lands correctly, nothing breaks?" question: YES. **Known side effect (owner decision pending):** running `--apply` twice (1st failed pre-fix, 2nd OK) left 12 duplicate `capacity` operational_facts for AZSEKER-CPC; verified INERT (0 active indicators reference `capacity`) so the demo is unaffected — the dedup DELETE was gated by the safety classifier → **user chose restore.** Restored the pre-E2E backup (`~/Documents/budgetpro-db-backups/budgetpro-pre-e2e-20260530-154814.sql`) atomically (`psql --single-transaction`, exit 0); DB verified pristine (732 lines / sortOrder=monthIndex / 1219 IVs / CPC 6,475,883), dev server rebooted clean (Ready 496ms, DB-querying API routes 200 → RLS intact). **Finding:** the 12 `capacity` dups were actually **pre-existing in the backup (predate the E2E)** + inert, so they persist harmlessly — never E2E-caused. The CF/#9/soft-delete CODE fixes are in git (`23002e4`) and unaffected by the DB DATA restore.

- **2026-05-30 (Demo-prep correctness sweep — #8 budgeting soft-delete + #9 month-bucketing root-cause; prod push HELD for release)** — Methodical full-release-quality pass ahead of the AzerSheker + FO Holding demos («не торопись чини всё — в конце видеть на проде»). Commits held on local `main` for a coordinated release push. **#8 — budgeting soft-delete consistency (`2859b8f` + `0f57519` + `8e6fe97`):** the recompute pipeline always filtered `deletedAt: null` (so the Risk Terminal + indicators were always correct), but the **budgeting DISPLAY + clone reads did not** — stale soft-deleted lines inflated P&L / analytics / export views. Added `deletedAt: null` to: analytics, lines, lines-count, export, snapshot, snapshot-actuals, cash-flow/plan-fact, fx-exposure, indicator drill-down, monthly-series, rolling (clone source + new-plan + forecast reads), plan-detail freeze, per-company dropdown, sync-actuals, and plan-diff (both sides → compares live versions). `availability.count` deliberately left unfiltered (coarse "does any data exist?" gate; sibling count models lack the column; result rarely differs). Handler-test assertions updated where they pinned the old `where` shape. **#9 — month-bucketing collapsed to January (MAJOR latent bug; `21e3ec3`):** `import-batch`'s `createMany` payload set `monthIndex` but **never `sortOrder`**, so every imported `BudgetLine` fell to the schema default `sortOrder=0`. The recompute resolver (`recompute-data-source.ts:312-327`), the Panel-3 drill-down (`indicators/values/[id]/drilldown`), and the 12-month series endpoint (`budget-lines/monthly-series`) ALL treat `sortOrder` — not `monthIndex` — as the 0..11 month index. With `sortOrder` pinned to 0: **monthly indicators lumped the entire year into 2026-01, quarterly into Q1, and Feb..Dec / Q2..Q4 read zero**; the drill-down returned zero source rows for any non-January month; the 12-month chart showed a January spike + flat zero. **Annual views masked it** (no `sortOrder` filter on year periods), which is why the default terminal looked fine — AzerSheker (all live data, `sortOrder=0`) was fully affected. **Fix:** the writer now mirrors `monthIndex` onto `sortOrder` (`r.monthIndex ?? 0`) — keeps all four readers consistent WITHOUT touching the core-calc bucketing logic; the sibling create paths (apply / apply-multi / budget / plans-clone / rolling / create-version) already set or preserved `sortOrder`, so `import-batch` was the sole buggy writer. **Live data repaired:** backfilled 545 live AzerSheker lines (`UPDATE sortOrder = monthIndex`) + recomputed via `recompute-fo-2026.ts` (6 companies, ok=105 / unknown=76 / failed=0). **DB-verified:** AZSEKER-CPC 2026-Q1 revenue (2,875,296) = exact sum of Jan(741,451)+Feb(959,158)+Mar(1,174,687); budget_lines now spread across `sortOrder` 0..3 with distinct per-month totals. **Containment confirmed:** the bug does NOT repeat in `balance_sheet_lines` / `cash_flow_entries` / `sales_budget_lines` / `cogs_budget_lines` — those store month in a dedicated `month Int NOT NULL` column (no defaultable field to silently collapse; DB shows sales 1..12, BS/CF 1..4 matching AzerSheker's 4-month coverage). 2 regression tests lock the writer invariant (`sortOrder == monthIndex`; null→0 fallback). tsc 0 throughout; affected suites green (import-batch 15/15). **Hardening (same day, follow-up commit):** the 3 month-readers (`recompute-data-source` / Panel-3 drill-down / 12-month series) now prefer `monthIndex` with a `sortOrder` fallback — coalesce-in-filter `OR: [{monthIndex: range}, {monthIndex: null, sortOrder: range}]` + `monthIndex ?? sortOrder` bucketing — matching the analytics/pnl readers, so month attribution no longer depends on the two columns staying synced (a future import path or hand-edit that mis-sets `sortOrder` can't silently re-collapse the months). **Proven no-op on current data:** re-recompute produced a byte-identical IV checksum (green 154/Σ340,467,085.74, red 55/Σ-23,189,206,868.22, amber 137/Σ1,494.94, unknown 452). 5 where-shape assertions repointed to the OR filter + 1 new row-resolution test (monthIndex wins; null→sortOrder; out-of-range→null); risk + drilldown + monthly-series suites 1784 green. *(Earlier punch-list #1-7 — plan-clone monthIndex, Panel-3 unit, board-deck weighting + cell-set — logged under 2026-05-29.)*

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 — post-deploy smoke test + F2 beacon fix + G3/F3 audit correction; commits `5bace59` code, this entry deferred from it)** — Built `deploy/smoke-test.sh` (answer to "что дальше" — de-risk the owner-gated deploy; `first-deploy.sh` provisions but nothing verified the result). One command, 8 unauth checks against a live URL: app-up, public NextAuth, page+API auth gates, F1 SSE gate, public beacon. **First run caught a real shipped-feature bug:** the Phase-8-F2 `/guide` view beacon (`POST /api/telemetry/guide-view`, meant to log ANONYMOUS views) was 401'd for anon — not by the route (genuinely public) but by **`src/proxy.ts`**, whose `publicPaths` omitted it. So zero anon `/guide` views were recorded since F2 shipped (28.05), dev AND prod. Fixed (added the path to `publicPaths`); smoke 8/8 green after; route test 6/6. **Bigger correction (the smoke test's real payoff):** `src/proxy.ts` IS the **Next 16 middleware** (renamed from `middleware.ts`; verified vs official docs it runs in prod incl. `output: standalone`) — so it already imposes a **global 401 on every non-public `/api/*`**. That overturns the G3 audit's "no middleware, per-handler only" claim AND explains the whole F3 saga: the two `middleware.ts` attempts failed only because Next 16 honors ONE middleware file (`proxy.ts`) — adding a second collides — NOT a NextAuth/Turbopack incompatibility. **F3 is therefore already satisfied** (global gate present; per-handler stays the verified authoritative source since Next 16 discourages auth-in-middleware; nginx `auth_request` is a bonus edge layer). `docs/AUTH_GATE_AUDIT.md` Method §2 + gate-taxonomy + F3 + conclusion + checklist all corrected; G2/G3 ROADMAP status flipped to ✅. The bug slipped past tests because the route's handler test bypasses `proxy.ts` and there's no proxy allowlist unit test — `deploy/smoke-test.sh` is now that guard. tsc 0; ✓ Next-16 proxy convention verified (Perplexity → nextjs.org).

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 — `criticalIndicator` default retargeted `*_NET_MARGIN` → `IND_EBITDA_MARGIN` so the alert actually fires; follow-up to `ec0e3bf`)** — `ec0e3bf` (a parallel session, same root-cause class as the Panel-4 fix) shipped the family-pattern *mechanism* for `critical-indicator-org-wide` — a leading `*` in `indicatorCode` suffix-matches every per-sector variant — and set the default to `*_NET_MARGIN`. **A live DB check this session surfaced that default is still structurally silent:** net margin is tracked by exactly **1** company holding-wide (`SVC_NET_MARGIN`, 0 red; `IND_/PHARMA_NET_MARGIN` inactive), but the threshold is **3 companies** — so `*_NET_MARGIN` can never reach it; it swapped a dead *exact* code for a structurally-dead *family*. **Fix:** default → `IND_EBITDA_MARGIN`, a generic profitability code that survived 7.M and is live + red across the holding (DB 2026-05-29: tracked by **6** companies, **4** red) → the rule fires *today*, which is the point of the task. EBITDA margin is also the cleaner holding-level profitability comparator than net margin (less financing/tax/one-off noise) across a diverse holding. The `*` family mechanism is RETAINED — any org can still opt into `*_NET_MARGIN` / `*_GROSS_MARGIN` / any suffix family via `Organization.settings.alertThresholds`; the *default* just isn't one. **Deliberate semantic change** to a config documented as "reproduce v1 behavior" — documented in `alert-thresholds-config.ts` + locked by the byte-for-byte default test. Also closed `ec0e3bf`'s noted low-priority sibling: stale `IND_GROSS_MARGIN` examples in `HelpModal.tsx:36` + `ScenarioFormModal.tsx:298` → live `IND_EBITDA_MARGIN`. **Decision basis:** user chose EBITDA over the net-margin family when shown the live-data table (net margin dormant vs EBITDA 4-red-today). Verified: tsc 0; `vitest run src/lib/risk` + board-deck snapshot = **1720 passed**; repointed default-path tests to EBITDA, pinned mechanism tests to an explicit `*_NET_MARGIN` config (no longer riding the default), added a lock test asserting the default is EBITDA-exact (NOT a net-margin family), switched the integration `buildFixture` org-wide trigger to EBITDA. (Context: `ec0e3bf` + `37103b6`/Sentry-G2 + F2 all landed on `main` during this turn — a parallel Phase-8 session is active; push held pending coordination.)

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 F2 — edge rate-limit recommendation + ready nginx config)** — Wrote `docs/RATE_LIMITING.md` closing the advisory half of the G3 F2 finding. Documents the current state (25/143 routes have per-route `enforceRateLimit`; the limiter is in-memory/per-process per `src/lib/rate-limit.ts:15`), then a production-ready nginx `limit_req` config: zone in `http {}` (the #1 placement mistake), `limit_req` on `/api/` (10 r/s, burst 20, nodelay) + a tighter `/api/auth/` (1 r/s, burst 5) for brute-force defense, `limit_req_status 429` (nginx defaults to 503 — verified, would've been wrong from memory), `ngx_http_realip_module` for the behind-CDN case (else all clients share one bucket), the TLS-block mirror note, starting thresholds, and `nginx -t` + curl-loop verify steps. Plus Vercel/Cloudflare alternatives if not behind our nginx. No code changed (doc only); applying it is a VM/edge action that can't be tested from the dev box. `docs/AUTH_GATE_AUDIT.md` F2 line updated to point at the doc. 🔍 nginx syntax single-source verified (Perplexity → nginx.org; Sonar out of credits).

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 G2 — Sentry wired DSN-ready, fully inert until a DSN is set)** — Installed `@sentry/nextjs` 10.55.0 (verified to support Next 16 + Turbopack) and wired the standard App-Router setup: `src/instrumentation.ts` (`register()` lazy-loads server/edge configs by runtime + exports `onRequestError = Sentry.captureRequestError`), `src/instrumentation-client.ts` (modern replacement for the legacy client config — required for Turbopack client instrumentation), `src/sentry.server.config.ts` + `src/sentry.edge.config.ts`, and `src/app/global-error.tsx` (root error boundary → `captureException`, inline-styled so it renders even when a CSS chunk is what failed). **Everything is DSN-gated**: each `Sentry.init` is wrapped in `if (dsn)`, and `next.config.ts` only applies `withSentryConfig` when `SENTRY_DSN`/`NEXT_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` is present — so with no DSN the build is byte-identical to before and there are zero network calls / global handlers (ZERO risk until opt-in). Set the DSN(s) per the new Sentry block in `.env.production.example` to activate, no code change. Errors-only by default (`tracesSampleRate` 0; bump via env). API surface verified against the *installed* package, not docs — `captureRouterTransitionStart` is NOT exported by 10.55.0 so it was omitted (would've been a hallucinated API). Verified: tsc 0; dev server restarted + booted clean (Ready 491ms, /login 200, no Sentry/instrumentation errors in log); inert next.config provably unchanged. Closes G2 in `docs/AUTH_GATE_AUDIT.md`. 🔍 setup cross-checked single-source (Perplexity → official docs.sentry.io; Sonar was out of credits).

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 — `criticalIndicator` alert rule fixed: dead exact code → per-sector family pattern)** — Follow-on to the Panel-4 fix, same root cause. The `critical-indicator-org-wide` alert ("a key metric is red for N+ companies = consolidated holding pressure") defaulted its `indicatorCode` to the exact `IND_NET_MARGIN`, matched via `ctx.indicators.find(i => i.code === code)` (alert-rules.ts:453). Phase 7.M moved margins to per-sector codes, so for AZSEKER that exact code matched **0 companies and the rule silently never fired**. **Fix:** `indicatorCode` now supports a "family" pattern — a leading `*` suffix-matches every variant (`*_NET_MARGIN` → FP_/SVC_/PHARMA_/IND_NET_MARGIN), default changed `IND_NET_MARGIN` → `*_NET_MARGIN`. A plain code (no `*`) stays an exact match (back-compat for orgs that pinned one indicator via `Organization.settings.alertThresholds`). Threshold now counts UNIQUE COMPANIES (identical to old cell-count in the single-indicator case → no regression; correct when the family spans sectors). `affectedIndicatorCodes` reports the real matched codes (e.g. `['FP_NET_MARGIN','SVC_NET_MARGIN']`); the message label strips the `*_` (`NET_MARGIN red for N companies`). Live check: `*_NET_MARGIN` indicators exist (IND_/SVC_/PHARMA_, 18 IVs) but 0 red right now, so the rule correctly stays silent — it will now fire when 3+ go red, which the old default never could. tsc 0; +4 unit tests (family matches per-sector code / aggregates across sectors / suffix-specific / exact-code back-compat) + updated the default + param assertions; alert suite 116 passing. Done inline (the spawned task chip can be dismissed). Low-priority sibling still open: stale `IND_GROSS_MARGIN` examples in HelpModal.tsx:36 + ScenarioFormModal.tsx:298 (illustrative only).

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 — board-deck visual-gate determinism fix; permanently-red gate → green-on-clean-code)** — Closed the board-deck sibling spec that the D1 closure flagged as red on clean code. Root cause was **data-drift, not layout-drift**: `visual-baseline-board-deck.spec.ts` masked the three LLM regions (headline / lead-ins / attribution) but left the composite **score digits** (`hero-score`) and the **contributing-count caption** (`Composite score · N of M`) unmasked — both recompute on ANY `IndicatorValue` change or sub-co add, so the pixel diff failed on every data change while the layout was untouched. A gate that's always red gives zero signal. **Fix:** added a `data-testid="hero-score-caption"` to the caption `<p>` (HeroSection.tsx) and added `hero-score` + `hero-score-caption` to the spec's mask set. Both are full-width block elements, so the mask is a fixed-width horizontal band: its y-offset + height still lock the score's vertical rhythm + font-size class (a `text-9xl→8xl` regression shrinks the band, still caught), but the digit VALUES no longer drift the baseline — determinism by construction (Playwright paints a solid `#FF00FF` rect over each region before capture, value-independent). Header doc rewritten to be honest about the new DOES/does-NOT-catch boundary (score block-position still locked; score micro-typography now unit-test territory, not pixel territory). Verified: HeroSection unit 16/16; regenerated baseline + 2 clean runs green (15.9s update, 6.2s verify); PNG visually confirmed — only the eyebrow `FO HOLDING` / `2026 · Review` renders as real text, every dynamic region is a solid mask band, zero score digits visible. The eyebrow year (`currentBakuYear()`) stays unmasked by choice — it rolls once a year (calendar event, not data-drift) and masking it would lose org-name/period alignment coverage; a 2027 baseline refresh is expected + trivial. `BASELINE UPDATE: board-deck-hero determinism — masked the composite score + caption (DATA-driven, recompute on every IndicatorValue change); this is NOT a layout change and NOT a data refresh — it stops the gate failing on score value churn while keeping all layout/frame/rhythm coverage.`

- **2026-05-29 (Phase 8 F1 — indicator catalog scoped to the org's active industries; multi-org-safe)** — Audit + fix. The *compute* path was already correctly multi-org-scoped (`matchCompaniesToIndicators` pairs a def with a company only when `industries` is empty or includes the company's industry), so the ~77 other-industry defs never produced values for AZSEKER and the "110 vs 33" was by-design multi-tenant catalog, not a bug — globally deactivating them would have broken a future hospitality/pharma org. The actual clutter was the catalog *list* endpoint `GET /api/indicators` (consumed by AlertRulesEditor + HotkeyToolbar), which returned all 110 regardless of the org's sectors. Fix: GET now derives the org's active industries from its companies (`company.findMany distinct industry`) and filters the catalog `where` to `industries isEmpty OR hasSome(orgIndustries)` — universal indicators always pass; a fresh org sees universal-only and widens as companies are onboarded. Query-time, no schema change, no compute/HeatMap change (the matrix already only showed relevant indicators). +1 handler test (distinct-industry pull + overlap-scope where). tsc 0 • full vitest 5356 green.

Replaced with a clean `new ArrayBuffer(byteLength) + Uint8Array.set()` copy that always produces a non-shared ArrayBuffer satisfying BodyInit. - **2026-05-28 (Phase 8 D3(j) — time-machine-utils.ts type tightening, 9 → 0 any + eslint-disable retired)** — Tenth file in the D3 incremental sweep. `src/lib/budgeting/time-machine-utils.ts` shipped with a file-level `eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any` and 9 untyped boundaries. Replaced with structural `TimeMachineLine` (id + plannedAmount? + forecastAmount? + accountId? + account?: { code? }) and `TimeMachineActual` (category + lineType + actualAmount) interfaces — narrowed to the columns the diff actually reads so any caller passing a real Prisma row satisfies the contract via duck typing. `getChangedCells` signature now type-safe end-to-end (4 typed array params, typed `Map<string, "increase"|"decrease"|"other">` output). `ChangeLogEntry.oldValue` / `newValue` / `snapshot` migrated from `any` to `unknown` — audit-log Json payload shapes vary per entityType + action, `unknown` forces callers to narrow before reading instead of silently bypassing the type system. `(prev[field] || 0)` swapped to `(prev[field] ?? 0)` since `field` is `as const` of `"plannedAmount" | "forecastAmount"` — both numeric, `0` is a legitimate value the `||` short-circuit would discard. Retired the file-level eslint-disable. Repo total `any` count: 347 → 163 (-184 across ten D3 files). tsc 0 • vitest 5330/5342 green (time-machine subset 14/14 specifically).

ROADMAP §2.1 ⬜→✅ across all 6 sub-items. **Verified:** tsc 0 throughout • full vitest **5172 → 5170 passing** (−2 from deleted helper wrapper tests; substantive code coverage unchanged) + 12 skipped. **Pre-import audit:** 21895 orphan rows (77% of BudgetLine + 100% of cogs/BS/CF) → Session 2 wipe → re-import via AI Auto Import → **100% FK coverage** on 5153 new ledger rows (4593 BudgetLine + 251 BS + 309 CF; cogs intentionally empty — table unused by modern import paths). **Architecture invariant:** ChartOfAccount is now the canonical, immutable account identity. Every ledger row joins back via FK; no String fallbacks exist. - **2026-05-26 (Phase 2.1 session 1 — AI Auto Import handlers auto-upsert CoA + populate accountId)** — **Session 1 of 4 in the D-plan multi-session Phase 2.1 cleanup.** Driver: 2026-05-26 audit revealed ROADMAP's "✅ Import writes accountId" was incorrect — only ~23% of BudgetLines and 0% of cogs/BS/CF had FK set, because AI Auto Import looked up `coaByCode.get("${entity}-${code}") ?? null` against a cache that contained codes WITHOUT the entity prefix. **3-commit sequence:** **(1) `3aa5d9d` CoA upsert helper** — new `src/lib/onboarding/upsert-chart-of-account.ts` (~110 LOC + 9 vitest cases). `resolveOrCreateAccountId(tx, cache, args)` idempotent upsert by (orgId, code) compound unique; new rows default to role='unknown' + accountType='expense' for admin reclassification via `/budgeting/admin/chart-of-accounts`. `createCoACache()` + `preWarmCoACache()` amortize the per-row cost. **(2) `902c399` PLF wire-up** — production-adapter-registry.ts `makePlfHandler` + dynamic-plf-adapter.ts both refactored to defer accountId resolution into applyToDb (where tx is available). Builds per-`line.code` spec map during sync row build, upserts unique codes inside applyToDb, attaches resolved id to each row. Account codes are now org-wide (no entity prefix on CoA lookup) — same `PLF.10.10.1` from AZSF and CPC resolves to one CoA row. **(3) `b9addba` BS+CF wire-up** — added optional `accountId` to BsImportRow + CfImportRow interfaces; bs-import-batch + cf-import-batch createMany payloads now thread the FK through; BS handler + dynamic-bs-adapter + CF handler + dynamic-cf-adapter all use same applyToDb upsert pattern. CF entryType maps to CoA accountType (inflow→revenue, outflow→expense). **Test infra:** 4 FAKE_TX fixtures + 1 prismaStub extended with `chartOfAccount.upsert` so handler tests can assert downstream payload shape without real Postgres. **Verified:** tsc 0 throughout • full vitest 5168 → 5172 passing (+4 from upsert helper tests) + 12 skipped • pre-commit M7 ✓ × 3 commits. **Effect:** NEW imports through any AI Auto Import path now write 100% FK coverage. Pre-existing 21895 orphan rows remain (will be wiped in Session 2). The "правильно" architectural fix is on its way; sessions 2-4 remain.

- **2026-05-26 (Phase 0.7 closure — CI workflow + scanner false-positive fix)** — **Phase 0.7 ✅ (status 🟡 → ✅).** GitHub Actions `.github/workflows/ci.yml` shipped: tsc + M7 status-band-shape scanner + secret-scan-over-all-tracked-files + Prisma generate + unit tests + Next.js production build, on push-to-main + PRs, ~6 min run. **Two commits:** **(1) `beefdfe` add workflow** — Node 20, ubuntu-latest, concurrency-group cancels stale runs; placeholder DATABASE_URL for build (Prisma client requires valid URL at init even though build doesn't query DB); intentionally NO lint step (Next.js 16 removed `next lint`; project has no standalone eslint config yet — separate cleanup), NO Playwright E2E (needs DB + dev server + LLM keys; future job once secrets land), NO Prisma migrate check (same blocker). **(2) `b05061f` scanner false-positive fix** — first CI run flagged `docker-compose.yml:45` `postgresql://${POSTGRES_USER}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}@db:5432/...` as a leak. Env-var interpolations are placeholder syntax, not credentials. Regex extended with zero-width negative lookahead on both user/password segments (`(?!\${)`); localhost/127.0.0.1 exception preserved. Scanner self-test gate 18/18 pass — non-placeholder credential detection unchanged. **Effect:** Every PR + main push now gated by 6 automated checks before merge. Latency identical to local pre-commit (~3-5s tsc + ~300ms M7) plus full vitest (~60s) and `next build` (~3-4 min). Bypass: nothing — CI cannot be skipped without admin merge override. **Verified:** [CI run #26419813971](https://github.com/rashadrahimov/leaddrive-budgeting/actions/runs/26419813971) all steps green in 6m13s. **Phase 0 status:** 6 of 7 tasks ✅ (only 0.1 admin password rotation remains 🟡, user-blocked on rotation strategy decision).

- **2026-05-22 (Phase 7.M — Dynamic PLF adapter: AI-powered structure detection fallback)** — **Any future P&L xlsx now imports without a hand-written adapter.** When the hard-coded AZSEKER parser returns 0 rows on a non-empty PLF sheet, `makePlfHandler` in `production-adapter-registry.ts` now delegates to `runDynamicPlfAdapter` — one LLM call detects the column layout, result cached 24h in `AIMapperProposalCache` (same-template re-uploads cost $0). **Files (3):** `dynamic-plf-adapter.ts` (NEW, ~215 LOC) — calls `extractMapperInput` → `getOrCreateProposal` (LLM+cache) → `resolveColumns` + `detectProposalYear` → walks rows matching `/^PLF\.\d{2}\.\d{2}\.([0-9]{1,2}|[A-Za-z]{1,2})$/`, applies PLF account-type classification + sign negate (not abs), builds `ImportBatchRow[]`, returns `AdapterRunResult` with `applyToDb` → `runImportBatch`. `production-adapter-registry.ts` (MODIFY, +15 LOC) — dynamic-fallback block after hard-coded loop: `if (rows.length === 0 && sheet && Object.keys(sheet).length > 1)`. `dynamic-plf-adapter.test.ts` (NEW, 9 tests) — mocks all external deps; covers extract error, confidence gate (<0.5), cache miss/hit warnings, partial proposal (missing months), PLF.10 skip + account-type classification, sign negate (not abs). **Cost:** AZSEKER files → $0 (hard-coded adapter handles); new format first upload → ~$0.05-0.10 one LLM call; subsequent → $0 (cache hit). **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean • `vitest run` **5059/5059 + 12 skipped**.

- **2026-05-21 (Phase 7.M Tier 7 Phase 6 — Route deprecation, Phase 6 of 6 import consolidation)** — **All three legacy import API routes now carry RFC deprecation signals; one is hard-gone (410).** Completes Phase 7.M Tier 7 Import Consolidation. **Route changes (3):** (1) `POST /api/budgeting/sales-forecast/import` — rewritten to **410 Gone** (zero UI callers confirmed in Phase 5 audit; SalesForecastTab uses inline PATCH on the non-import endpoint). Returns RFC 8594 Sunset + RFC 9745 Deprecation + RFC 8288 Link headers with `error`, `replacement`, `ui`, `replacedAt`, `phase` JSON body. (2) `POST /api/operational-facts/import` — wrapped with `withDeprecation({ replacedBy: "/api/import/ai-auto-multi", reason: "OPS_FACTS shape..." })` HOF; route stays **fully functional** with Sunset/Deprecation/Link/X-Replaced-By headers on every response. (3) `POST /api/budgeting/import-csv` — same `withDeprecation` wrapping; route stays **fully functional** (GET list-history endpoint unchanged). **New helper** `src/lib/api-deprecation.ts` (95 LOC) — `applyDeprecationHeaders(response, opts): void` mutates NextResponse/Response in-place; `withDeprecation(opts)(handler): H` HOF wraps handler, applies headers post-response; default sunset `"Thu, 21 May 2026 00:00:00 GMT"`. **Test changes (3 files):** `operational-facts/import/handler.test.ts` +1 test (stamps Deprecation/Sunset/Link/X-Replaced-By headers); `budgeting/import-csv/handler.test.ts` +1 test (same); `budgeting/sales-forecast/import/handler.test.ts` **rewritten** (old auth/validation tests replaced by 3 new tests locking 410 status, body shape, and deprecation headers). **Bug fix:** em-dash U+2014 in reason string caused ByteString conversion error (HTTP headers are ASCII-only) — replaced with ASCII hyphen. **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean • `vitest run` **5050/5050 + 12 skipped** (+3 tests from Phase 5 baseline 5047). **Effect:** Any client (curl, browser, integration) hitting the legacy import routes now receives machine-readable signals (Sunset header = when to stop, Deprecation flag = already deprecated, Link = where to go). The operational-facts and CSV-import forms continue to work as before — deprecation is advisory, not enforcement. The sales-forecast import route is fully removed (was already unreachable from UI).

- **2026-05-21 (Phase 7.M Tier 7 — SALES_FORECAST dataType, Phase 4 of 5 import consolidation)** — **AI Auto Import now covers department×month sales-forecast grid xlsx** (col 1 = department label, cols 2-13 = Jan..Dec amounts). Replaces `/api/budgeting/sales-forecast/import` (111 LOC standalone ExcelJS route) with one classifier branch + adapter + new batch fn. Distinct from PLF (whose rows are P&L line items: Revenue/COGS/EBITDA) and from existing SALES (which writes operational_facts via Azik KPI handler) — SALES_FORECAST writes `SalesForecast` table (org-scoped, no plan dependency, unique by `(orgId, deptId, year, month)`). **Files (6 src + 4 test):** `sheet-classifier.ts` adds SALES_FORECAST to union + `VALID_DATA_TYPES`; `sheet-classifier-system.ts` adds SALES_FORECAST branch to system prompt with department-label-vs-P&L-line-item distinction signal; `file-type-detector.ts` adds `salesForecast` counter + bucket + new `"sales-forecast"` FileType + Priority 5.8 branch; `multi-file-orchestrator.ts` adds `"sales-forecast": 1.7` to APPLY_ORDER + empty-counts literal entry; `production-adapter-registry.ts` adds (a) `OrgContext.deptLabelToId` field + lazy load of revenue-generating departments in `resolveOrgContext` (mirrors `/api/budgeting/sales-forecast/import` filter `hasRevenue=true && isActive=true`), (b) `makeSalesForecastHandler` (~85 LOC) that resolves dept-label-case-insensitive → departmentId via ctx, warns once per unknown label (deduplicated), calls `runSalesForecastBatch` with the outer multi-file tx; test stub `buildPrismaStub` extended with `budgetDepartment.findMany`. **NEW pure module** `sales-forecast-import.ts` (130 LOC) — positional grid parser: col 0 = label (lowercased trimmed), cols 1-12 = months 1-12, skips Total/Итого/Cəmi/Ümumi labels, includes explicit zeros as legitimate amounts, warns on non-finite/negative cells. **NEW batch fn** `sales-forecast-batch.ts` (170 LOC) — fifth sister of `runImportBatch`/`runBalanceSheetBatch`/`runCashFlowBatch`/`runKpiBatch`/`runActualsBatch`. UPSERT semantics by (orgId, deptId, year, month) unique key — no RESET phase (mirrors legacy upsert loop). Reconciliation per `(departmentId, month)`. Accepts PrismaClient OR Prisma.TransactionClient. **Tests: +22 new** (11 parser + 4 batch + 1 classifier accept + 3 file-type-detector + 3 handler). **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean • `vitest run` **5047/5047 + 12 skipped** (Phase 3 baseline was 5025). **Effect:** Finance can drop a department×month sales-forecast xlsx into `/budgeting/admin/ai-import` instead of `/api/budgeting/sales-forecast/import`. Old route stays live until Phase 6 deprecation. Phase 5 (UI consolidation) next — rename `/budgeting/admin/ai-import` page and remove 3 old sidebar entries.

- **2026-05-21 (Phase 7.M Tier 7 — BUDGET_ACTUALS dataType, Phase 3 of 5 import consolidation)** — **AI Auto Import now covers flat budget-actuals xlsx** (category|amount|date|department|description|lineType|companyCode shape). Replaces `/api/budgeting/import-csv` (204 LOC standalone) with one classifier branch + adapter + new batch fn. Distinct from PLF (which has month columns spanning Jan-Dec) — BUDGET_ACTUALS has DATE in a column, one row per transaction. Writes to `budget_actuals` table (separate from BudgetLine/PLF) via new `runActualsBatch`. **Files (5 src + 4 test):** `sheet-classifier.ts` adds BUDGET_ACTUALS to union + `VALID_DATA_TYPES`; `sheet-classifier-system.ts` adds BUDGET_ACTUALS branch to system prompt (distinguishes from PLF by row-shape vs month-column shape signal); `file-type-detector.ts` adds `budgetActuals` counter + bucket + new `"budget-actuals"` FileType + Priority 5.7 branch (mixed-with-financial falls through to main-financial); `multi-file-orchestrator.ts` adds `"budget-actuals": 1.5` to APPLY_ORDER (right after main-financial since actuals need a BudgetPlan to attach to); `production-adapter-registry.ts` adds `makeBudgetActualsHandler` (~95 LOC) that wraps the target sheet for `parseBudgetActualsWorkbook`, resolves optional `companyCode → companyId` via shared org context, filters year-mismatch rows, and calls `runActualsBatch` with the outer multi-file tx. **NEW pure module** `budget-actuals-import.ts` (256 LOC) — header-alias parser with DD.MM.YYYY + Excel serial date support, ABS-value semantics matching `/api/budgeting/import-csv`, validation (missing category / non-finite amount / malformed date) into errors+warnings split. **NEW batch fn** `actuals-import-batch.ts` (216 LOC) — fourth sister of `runImportBatch` (PLF) / `runBalanceSheetBatch` (BS) / `runCashFlowBatch` (CF) / `runKpiBatch` (KPI). REPLACE semantics by (planId, dateScope year prefix or explicit dates) — purges prior actuals in scope before bulk-insert. Reconciliation per `(planId, category, monthIndex)` keys via shared `reconcile()` helper. Accepts PrismaClient OR Prisma.TransactionClient (multi-file outer-tx path) — same dual-mode pattern as the sibling batches. **Tests: +26 new** (13 parser unit + 5 batch-fn unit + 1 classifier accept + 3 file-type-detector + 4 handler). **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean • `vitest run` **5025/5025 + 12 skipped** (baseline Phase 2 was 4999). **Effect:** Finance can now drop any flat budget-actuals xlsx into `/budgeting/admin/ai-import` instead of using `/api/budgeting/import-csv`. - **2026-05-12** — **Phase 7.H Feature 4.v2.4 — SASB materiality matrix (closes the F4 family).** v2.1 (provenance badge) + v2.2 (industry factors) + v2.2.1 (confidence) + v2.3 (manual disclosure) covered "where does the number come from / how trustworthy is it / how to enter the real one." v2.4 closes the last gap: **not every ESG indicator matters for every sector**. SASB Materiality Map says: services/education × Scope 1 = not material (no direct combustion); real_estate / retail × Scope 1 = low materiality (HVAC dominates, not direct emissions); industrial/agro × Scope 1 = highly material. v2.4 surfaces this distinction so the heatmap stops showing equal visual weight on cells that don't matter. **Stack:** new [src/lib/risk/esg-materiality.ts](src/lib/risk/esg-materiality.ts) — sparse-override catalog (14 sectors × 5 ESG indicators, default = `material`). `MaterialityRating` union: `material` | `low_materiality` | `not_material`. Calibration sourced from SASB Materiality Finder + MSCI ESG Industry Materiality Map 2023; each override carries a human-readable note (e.g. "Campus operations dominated by purchased electricity (Scope 2), not direct combustion."). Helper `isMaterialityScoped(code)` gates the framework to the 5 ESG codes; financial/operational indicators are universally material so the matrix doesn't apply. **APIs:** `/api/indicators/matrix` builds per-(companyId, indicatorId) materiality at cell-emission time (pre-built lookup maps keep it O(1)); op-cells + real-rollup parent cells both carry the tag. `/api/indicators/values/[id]` adds `materiality` + `materialityNote` for the Panel-3 tooltip. **UI:** [HeatMap.tsx](src/features/terminal/components/HeatMap.tsx) — `low_materiality` → 0.45× base opacity (legible, status color preserved, drill-downable); `not_material` → 0.12× opacity + status color stripped to neutral gray `#1F2937` (effectively "this metric doesn't apply"). Cell `aria-label` extended with rating for screen readers. [IndicatorDetail.tsx](src/features/terminal/components/IndicatorDetail.tsx) — new `MaterialityBadge` component renders next to the provenance badge: amber-soft «НИЗКАЯ МАТЕРИАЛЬНОСТЬ» or neutral-gray «НЕ МАТЕРИАЛЬНО». `material` (default) + null (non-ESG) render nothing — adding "MATERIAL" on every cell would be visual noise. Tooltip surfaces SASB explanation + per-pair calibration note. **i18n:** 3 keys × 3 locales (`materiality.lowMateriality` / `notMaterial` / `tooltipSuffix`). **Tests:** new `esg-materiality.test.ts` (17 cases: canonical SASB calls — services × Scope 1 = not_material, industrial × Scope 1 = material; defensive defaults — null/unknown industry → material; note presence for non-default; gate scope = 5 ESG codes only) + `IndicatorDetail.test.tsx` extended (+4 cases: not_material badge + tooltip, low_materiality badge, material → no badge, null → no badge). Full vitest **3079 → 3100/3100** (+21); tsc 0. **End-to-end browser verify (preview :3003):** API correctly tags LLS-MAIN (services) × IND_CARBON_SCOPE_1 as `materiality: 'not_material'` and ATL-DBZ (industrial) × Scope 1 as `material`; HeatMap shows 59 material + 4 low_materiality + 2 not_material cells; Panel 3 on LLS-MAIN × Scope 1 displays **two stacked badges** (ОТРАСЛЕВАЯ ОЦЕНКА B + НЕ МАТЕРИАЛЬНО) with full tooltip "SASB-материальность: показатель не приоритетен для этой отрасли. No direct combustion / production heat. Scope 1 effectively zero." **F4 family complete:** клиент теперь видит на каждой ESG-ячейке (1) откуда цифра берётся [v2.1], (2) насколько модель достоверна [v2.2.1], (3) можно ли её исправить ручным вводом [v2.3], (4) важна ли вообще эта метрика для этого сектора [v2.4]. Phase 7.H Feature 4 закрыт.
- **2026-05-12** — **Phase 7.H Feature 4.v2.2.1 — confidence-tier persistence (closes the v2.2 ladder).** v2.2 catalog already knew each (industry × scope) confidence (`A`|`B`|`C`|`D`) but didn't persist it; `IndicatorValue.confidence` column (added in v2.1 migration) sat null. This turn extracts the worst-tier across scopes used by the formula, threads it through `upsertIndicatorValue`, persists on every modeled-industry IV, surfaces it as a glyph on the Panel-3 badge + tooltip. **Stack:** new `worstConfidenceFromAggregate(industry_factor)` helper in [recompute.ts](src/lib/risk/recompute.ts) (rule: composite of Scope 1 (B) + Scope 2 (B) + Scope 3 (C) lands at C — only as trustworthy as weakest scope); `RecomputeDataSource.upsertIndicatorValue` extended with `confidence?: 'A'|'B'|'C'|'D'|null`; Prisma adapter writes confidence on CREATE and UPDATE (caller's null clears the tier — disclosed override flips to ground-truth). Disclosed cells skip the tier (truth, not model). Pure-budgetLine financial cells stay null (industryFactor not in requiredInputs → no aggregate → no tier). **UI:** `ProvenanceBadge` extended with `confidence` prop — appends bold tier glyph next to label (e.g. «ОТРАСЛЕВАЯ ОЦЕНКА B»), tooltip surfaces "Доверие модели: B (A — лучшее, D — худшее)". i18n key `provenance.confidenceLabel` added to en/ru/az. **Tests:** 4 new cases in [recompute.test.ts](src/lib/risk/recompute.test.ts) — industry-modeled Scope 1 alone → B (matches agro_crops catalog tier); ESG composite Scope 1+2+3 → C (worst of B,B,C); disclosed override clears tier; financial indicator stays null. Full vitest **3075 → 3079/3079** (+4 net; +14 new minus +10 fixture-overlap); tsc 0. **End-to-end browser verify (preview :3003):** full recompute fired (199 IVs, 159 ok); DB query confirms `confidence='B'` on every modeled-industry carbon IV across 8 ops companies, NULL on AZSEKER-EDEN's disclosed cell; UI shows «ОТРАСЛЕВАЯ ОЦЕНКА B» badge on ATL-DBZ (49.6K red), no tier glyph on AZSEKER-EDEN (disclosed 800 green). Closes the v2.2 ladder — clients see at-a-glance both what kind of estimate the number is AND how trustworthy the underlying model is.
- **2026-05-12** — **Phase 7.H Feature 4.v2.2 — industry-specific intensity factors for ESG (retires the generic 0.5 placeholder).** User asked «что дальше» after v2.3 closure. Right next step: kill the misleading single-coefficient ESG values that produce the same 25K placeholder for every company regardless of sector. v2.2 swaps `revenue × 0.5 / 1000` for `revenue × industryFactor("scope_N") / 1000`, where the factor is sector-specific (agro_crops=0.08, industrial=0.45, services=0.02, etc.). **Catalog:** new [src/lib/risk/industry-emission-factors.ts](src/lib/risk/industry-emission-factors.ts) — 14 sector rows × 3 scopes (`scope_1`, `scope_2`, `scope_3`) × confidence tier (`A`|`B`|`C`|`D`) + calibration note per row. Sources: IPCC AR6, EPA Emission Factors Hub, DEFRA 2023 conversion factors, MSCI Climate Indices sector averages, CDP A-List. **Resolver:** new `industryFactorResolver` in [recompute.ts](src/lib/risk/recompute.ts) — pre-resolves every `industryFactor:<scope>` listed in `requiredInputs`, injects synchronous `industryFactor(scope)` function into the formula engine, stamps `state.inputs.aggregates.industry_factor` snapshot for the drilldown UI. Fail-loud on unknown industry — returns NaN → `status='unknown'` rather than silent zero. **Seeds rewritten:** 4 ESG formulas now consume the new resolver; `defaultValueSource` flipped from `modeled_generic` → `modeled_industry`; thresholds recalibrated for the new value scale (Scope 1: green<2K, amber<15K, red≥15K vs v2.1's 1K/5K). **Provenance ladder enforced:** `disclosed > modeled_industry > modeled_generic > macro > computed` — disclosed v2.3 entries continue to override the new industry model; UI badge auto-selects the right colour ("Отраслевая оценка" amber for industry-modeled). **Trigger wiring:** `industry` threaded through `IndicatorDefinitionLike` → `recomputeIndicator` → `buildContext` → `ResolverCtx`. `recompute-trigger.ts` + `/api/indicators` (sync + async branches) pass `company.industry` to every pair. **Seed-time validator:** `validateRequiredInputs` now rejects `industryFactor:foobar` with a clear scope-name error message — catches typos before they hit production. **Tests:** new `industry-emission-factors.test.ts` (12 cases: catalog coverage vs `industries/data.ts`, factor sanity (≥0, finite, Scope 3 ≥ Scope 1, within 0..5 kg/AZN), lookup helper) + `esg-seeds.test.ts` upgraded (formulas reference `industryFactor()`, requiredInputs declare scopes, defaultValueSource = modeled_industry) + `recompute.test.ts` extended with 6 new cases (agro_crops → amber 4K, industrial → red 22.5K, services → green 1K, unknown industry → status=unknown, null industry → unknown, disclosure beats industry factor). Full vitest **3054 → 3075/3075** (+21); tsc 0. **End-to-end browser verify (preview :3003):** seed re-run, `POST /api/indicators?period=2026` async batch fan-out completed 159 OK + 40 unknown (40 = sectors without industry tagged on company, expected). Matrix sample: AZSEKER-EDEN (agro, disclosed override held) → 800 tCO2e green "РАСКРЫТО"; ATL-DBZ (industrial) → 49.6K red "ОТРАСЛЕВАЯ ОЦЕНКА" (was 25K under generic); LLS-MAIN (services) → 22 tCO2e green (was 25K under generic — services factor 0.02 vs 0.5). Same indicator now produces materially different numbers per sector AND wears the right provenance badge. **Roadmap forward:** confidence-tier persistence (A/B/C/D writes into `IndicatorValue.confidence` from the resolver aggregate) deferred to v2.2.1 follow-up; materiality matrix (v2.4 — hide irrelevant ESG per sector) still in queue.

This turn ships both. **Safety rails (the «чтоб клиент случайно не ошибся» part):** new pure module `src/lib/risk/metric-validation-rules.ts` ships 21 operational metric rules (covering all 13 KPI indicators across 7 sectors) + 4 ESG disclosure rules. Each rule pins unit (so kg≠tonnes), hard min/max (rejected as 400), soft warnMin/warnMax (UI confirm dialog), and `anomalyDeltaPct` (extra confirm when value > X% from this company's 12-row historical mean). `validateValue()` returns errors / warnings / anomalyWarning separately; the API only persists when client passes `forceConfirm: true` on the second call. **Stack:** new Prisma `IndicatorDisclosure` model (companyId × indicatorCode × period uniqueness, applied via `/tmp/claude/disclosure_migration.sql`), new audit-action enum values (`operational_fact_*` + `indicator_disclosure_*`), `RecomputeDataSource.getIndicatorDisclosure()` method added to the interface, `recomputeIndicator()` now reads disclosure FIRST and skips formula evaluation if a row exists, stamping `valueSource: 'disclosed'` (overrides the seed's `modeled_generic`). **APIs:** `/api/operational-facts` (GET list + POST upsert + admin DELETE, manager+ writes, Zod-validated, anomaly + soft-bound confirm-required pattern, audit-event on every mutation) + `/api/indicator-disclosures` (same shape, plus fires targeted `runRecomputeForCompanies` on save so the badge auto-flips without manual recompute). **UI:** `/budgeting/admin/data-entry` with Operational + ESG tabs at [DataEntryAdmin.tsx](src/features/budgeting/components/DataEntryAdmin.tsx); metric selector restricted to catalog (no free-form), unit prefilled per metric, source-note + date + value fields with hint text from the rule, confirm-required dialog on warnings. Closes user's gap «другие там еще есть индикаторы которые засветили информацию» for the 4 ESG codes; operational KPIs (yield/occupancy/etc.) now have an entry path though their resolvers will still return `unknown` until clients actually enter data. **Roadmap forward:** v2.2 (industry-intensity factor for un-disclosed cells), v2.4 (SASB materiality matrix) remain in the queue.
